# OCADE — exhaustive security remediation and negative-test checklist

Source SHA: `166328b973ee2533356fcbdc92d946a783fd9a2e` · Branch: `ocade/p00-p22-implementation`
Sources: [192 root risks and 1,152 negative scenarios](https://github.com/BREVEX-CEO/caera-connect-plan/blob/166328b973ee2533356fcbdc92d946a783fd9a2e/docs/OCADE_SECURITY_THREAT_MODEL_AND_NEGATIVE_TEST_REGISTER.md#L88) · [triage status and 16 observed findings](https://github.com/BREVEX-CEO/caera-connect-plan/blob/166328b973ee2533356fcbdc92d946a783fd9a2e/docs/OCADE_THREAT_TRIAGE.md#L9)

> **Status rule:** Recorded FIXED/PARTIAL/OPEN/OWNER/N/A are prior triage states, not newly verified outcomes. Every checkbox starts unchecked pending exact-SHA-specific evidence. The 192 risks are hypotheses, not 192 confirmed exploitable vulnerabilities; the 1,152 cases are proposed tests, not results. Record justified OWNER/N/A outcomes, and never call an untested negative case PASS.

## Security entry and exit gates
- [ ] Review all 16 O-01–O-16 code-observed findings against current code and authorization boundaries.
- [ ] Independently triage all 192 root risks; assign exact implementation/test owners and source links.
- [ ] For each applicable risk, reproduce when possible, implement its mitigation without weakening policy, and verify positive and negative behavior.
- [ ] For each of 1,152 negative scenarios, record executable fixture/test, exact SHA, environment, observed result and failure containment.
- [ ] Test two-tenant isolation on every API, storage, cache, memory, model call, connector, worker and export with account-swapped canaries.
- [ ] Verify malicious uploads, MCP/skills sandboxing, prompt injection, secrets and unauthorized network/filesystem effects are safely blocked.
- [ ] Verify STOP, Take Over, approval scope, unknown-effect non-replay, idempotency, concurrency, safe browser/desktop targeting and quota protection.
- [ ] Recheck historical FIXED labels with independent evidence on final code; do not infer verification from old CI.
- [ ] Resolve applicable PARTIAL/OPEN findings; record owner-only platform/configuration blockers and prove any N/A designation.
- [ ] Run isolated coding/security negative tests before debloat, real-model benchmarks or cloud-computer evaluation.
- [ ] Require zero known untriaged or release-blocking applicable security defects and completed passing mandatory checks on the SAME final SHA.

## Code-observed findings O-01–O-16

- [ ] **O-01** — earlier triage: **FIXED** · Evidence: `online-client.ts` no longer claims vision from reachability; the server decides per model (`edge-handler.ts` `vision`, `x-ocade-vision`); `tests/security/model-route.test.ts` · Remaining gap: none recorded. [Source](https://github.com/BREVEX-CEO/caera-connect-plan/blob/166328b973ee2533356fcbdc92d946a783fd9a2e/docs/OCADE_THREAT_TRIAGE.md#L13)
- [ ] **O-02** — earlier triage: **FIXED** · Evidence: images reach only a model that sees them (declared, Google API, or BYOK test proved); a text-only model gets a note, no previews, no `ui.click_point` (`planner.server.ts` `plannerSight`); `model-route.test.ts`, `tests/jarvis/contracts.test.ts` · Remaining gap: auxiliary vision/OCR for text-only models not built. [Source](https://github.com/BREVEX-CEO/caera-connect-plan/blob/166328b973ee2533356fcbdc92d946a783fd9a2e/docs/OCADE_THREAT_TRIAGE.md#L14)
- [ ] **O-03** — earlier triage: **FIXED** · Evidence: Settings > BYOK, Vault-backed connections, per-surface choice, per-task source, no OCADE fallback, permit not charged (`20261117130000`); `byok.test.sql`, `byok-route.test.ts`, `byok-settings.test.ts` · Remaining gap: not deployed as Edge Functions on staging; not qualified against a real provider. [Source](https://github.com/BREVEX-CEO/caera-connect-plan/blob/166328b973ee2533356fcbdc92d946a783fd9a2e/docs/OCADE_THREAT_TRIAGE.md#L15)
- [ ] **O-04** — earlier triage: **PARTIAL** · Evidence: `library/intake.ts` refuses programs, scripts, macro documents, active PDFs and archive bombs before storage; `library-intake.test.ts` · Remaining gap: no antivirus engine or content disarm; the product does not claim a virus scan. [Source](https://github.com/BREVEX-CEO/caera-connect-plan/blob/166328b973ee2533356fcbdc92d946a783fd9a2e/docs/OCADE_THREAT_TRIAGE.md#L16)
- [ ] **O-05** — earlier triage: **PARTIAL** · Evidence: static scan plus quarantine (`catalog.py`); external servers get no workspace and a minimal environment · Remaining gap: no OS-level filesystem/network confinement for external MCP processes. [Source](https://github.com/BREVEX-CEO/caera-connect-plan/blob/166328b973ee2533356fcbdc92d946a783fd9a2e/docs/OCADE_THREAT_TRIAGE.md#L17)
- [ ] **O-06** — earlier triage: **PARTIAL** · Evidence: Windows workers drop all privileges but change-notify (`sandbox.run_restricted`) · Remaining gap: Administrators group kept on an elevated companion; macOS commands not sandboxed. [Source](https://github.com/BREVEX-CEO/caera-connect-plan/blob/166328b973ee2533356fcbdc92d946a783fd9a2e/docs/OCADE_THREAT_TRIAGE.md#L18)
- [ ] **O-07** — earlier triage: **PARTIAL** · Evidence: independent steps run in up to four lanes at once; the database derives resources and refuses overlaps; free agents get independent work while others run; STOP/Take Over fence every lane (`parallel-lanes.test.sql`, `lanes.test.ts`, `adaptive-team.test.ts`, `test_lanes.py`) · Remaining gap: no hosted Windows/macOS proof on the final SHA (Stage 4); no measured solo-vs-team comparison (Stage 5). [Source](https://github.com/BREVEX-CEO/caera-connect-plan/blob/166328b973ee2533356fcbdc92d946a783fd9a2e/docs/OCADE_THREAT_TRIAGE.md#L19)
- [ ] **O-08** — earlier triage: **PARTIAL** · Evidence: one model per task; BYOK failures are refused, never moved to OCADE (`byok-route.test.ts`) · Remaining gap: no opt-in fallback policy is offered (fallback simply never happens). [Source](https://github.com/BREVEX-CEO/caera-connect-plan/blob/166328b973ee2533356fcbdc92d946a783fd9a2e/docs/OCADE_THREAT_TRIAGE.md#L20)
- [ ] **O-09** — earlier triage: **OPEN** · Evidence:  · Remaining gap: real-model and weak-model evaluations blocked by the Part 23 gate. [Source](https://github.com/BREVEX-CEO/caera-connect-plan/blob/166328b973ee2533356fcbdc92d946a783fd9a2e/docs/OCADE_THREAT_TRIAGE.md#L21)
- [ ] **O-10** — earlier triage: **PARTIAL** · Evidence: RLS on every public table (`injection-privilege.test.sql`), two-person tests for BYOK, memory and model-call records · Remaining gap: a two-tenant matrix over every route, storage URL and cache not built. [Source](https://github.com/BREVEX-CEO/caera-connect-plan/blob/166328b973ee2533356fcbdc92d946a783fd9a2e/docs/OCADE_THREAT_TRIAGE.md#L22)
- [ ] **O-11** — earlier triage: **PARTIAL** · Evidence: server-side permits, claim and settle (`generation-gate.ts`, DB tests) · Remaining gap: replay/race harness against DevTools tampering not built. [Source](https://github.com/BREVEX-CEO/caera-connect-plan/blob/166328b973ee2533356fcbdc92d946a783fd9a2e/docs/OCADE_THREAT_TRIAGE.md#L23)
- [ ] **O-12** — earlier triage: **PARTIAL** · Evidence: every report names its exact SHA; the known-vulnerability gate is green (below) · Remaining gap: the same-SHA campaign for the final coding SHA is pending (Stage 4). [Source](https://github.com/BREVEX-CEO/caera-connect-plan/blob/166328b973ee2533356fcbdc92d946a783fd9a2e/docs/OCADE_THREAT_TRIAGE.md#L24)
- [ ] **O-13** — earlier triage: **OPEN** · Evidence: one shared native capture per window with change regions and ordered keyframes (`capture.py`, `test_capture.py`); template matching for clicks (`vision.py`) · Remaining gap: still no semantic local visual parser; meaning comes from accessibility or a vision-capable model. [Source](https://github.com/BREVEX-CEO/caera-connect-plan/blob/166328b973ee2533356fcbdc92d946a783fd9a2e/docs/OCADE_THREAT_TRIAGE.md#L25)
- [ ] **O-14** — earlier triage: **FIXED** · Evidence: `gateway-address.ts`: HTTPS on 443 only, no credentials/query/fragment, no IP literal in any encoding, no internal names, every resolved IPv4/IPv6 (mapped, NAT64) public; redirects refused; the platform checks certificates; `gateway-address.test.ts` · Remaining gap: DNS rebinding between the check and the connection is mitigated by certificate validation, not by pinning the dialled IP. [Source](https://github.com/BREVEX-CEO/caera-connect-plan/blob/166328b973ee2533356fcbdc92d946a783fd9a2e/docs/OCADE_THREAT_TRIAGE.md#L26)
- [ ] **O-15** — earlier triage: **PARTIAL** · Evidence: as O-04 · Remaining gap: as O-04. [Source](https://github.com/BREVEX-CEO/caera-connect-plan/blob/166328b973ee2533356fcbdc92d946a783fd9a2e/docs/OCADE_THREAT_TRIAGE.md#L27)
- [ ] **O-16** — earlier triage: **FIXED** · Evidence: staged MCP updates: validation, pins, scan, live tool-list health check, permission diff approval, previous kept, authorization log (`catalog.promote`/`rollback`, `ocade_agent mcp-update`); `test_catalog.StagedUpdates` · Remaining gap: no website UI for it. [Source](https://github.com/BREVEX-CEO/caera-connect-plan/blob/166328b973ee2533356fcbdc92d946a783fd9a2e/docs/OCADE_THREAT_TRIAGE.md#L28)

## All 192 root-risk remediations and their 1,152 negative tests

### AUTH: Authentication and account recovery (8 roots; 48 negative tests)

- [ ] **AUTH-01** — prior triage **PARTIAL** · Risk: Password recovery link can be reused after successful recovery. Mitigation: Single-use token redemption, rotation of all sessions and replay-negative test. · Remaining: other sessions are not ended after a recovery (provider setting); run the script on staging. [Risk](https://github.com/BREVEX-CEO/caera-connect-plan/blob/166328b973ee2533356fcbdc92d946a783fd9a2e/docs/OCADE_SECURITY_THREAT_MODEL_AND_NEGATIVE_TEST_REGISTER.md#L94) · [Triage](https://github.com/BREVEX-CEO/caera-connect-plan/blob/166328b973ee2533356fcbdc92d946a783fd9a2e/docs/OCADE_THREAT_TRIAGE.md#L44)
  - [ ] **OT-01-01-01** — For password recovery link can be reused after successful recovery, login with an identifier belonging to a second account. **Pass condition:** Deny or safely contain the negative condition; enforce: single-use token redemption, rotation of all sessions and replay-negative test; no unauthorized external effect, tenant disclosure, hidden model charge, or false completion. [Case](https://github.com/BREVEX-CEO/caera-connect-plan/blob/166328b973ee2533356fcbdc92d946a783fd9a2e/docs/OCADE_SECURITY_THREAT_MODEL_AND_NEGATIVE_TEST_REGISTER.md#L410)
  - [ ] **OT-01-01-02** — For password recovery link can be reused after successful recovery, perform operation after password reset and old token survival. **Pass condition:** Deny or safely contain the negative condition; enforce: single-use token redemption, rotation of all sessions and replay-negative test; no unauthorized external effect, tenant disclosure, hidden model charge, or false completion. [Case](https://github.com/BREVEX-CEO/caera-connect-plan/blob/166328b973ee2533356fcbdc92d946a783fd9a2e/docs/OCADE_SECURITY_THREAT_MODEL_AND_NEGATIVE_TEST_REGISTER.md#L411)
  - [ ] **OT-01-01-03** — For password recovery link can be reused after successful recovery, submit two conflicting authentication requests simultaneously. **Pass condition:** Deny or safely contain the negative condition; enforce: single-use token redemption, rotation of all sessions and replay-negative test; no unauthorized external effect, tenant disclosure, hidden model charge, or false completion. [Case](https://github.com/BREVEX-CEO/caera-connect-plan/blob/166328b973ee2533356fcbdc92d946a783fd9a2e/docs/OCADE_SECURITY_THREAT_MODEL_AND_NEGATIVE_TEST_REGISTER.md#L412)

### TENANT: Cross-user data and account isolation (8 roots; 48 negative tests)

- [ ] **TENANT-01** — prior triage **PARTIAL** · Risk: Object ID access can cross tenant boundary despite authentication. ### BROWSER: Browser, DOM, navigation and web automation (8 roots; 48 negative tests)

- [ ] **BROWSER-01** — prior triage **FIXED** · Risk: Browser action can target a stale or duplicated selector, including a previously learned workflow replayed after an app/site update. Mitigation: Check fresh ref/version, unique target and current app/site identity before every mutation; requalify saved procedures and quarantine workflows whose targets or effects have drifted. · Remaining: —. ## Final security proof
- [ ] Recalculate outcomes for 24 domains × 8 risks = 192 root cases and 6 adversarial scenarios/root = 1,152 test records.
- [ ] Quantify repeated/race/denial tests; independently inspect accounts, real side effects and persisted output.
- [ ] Maintain exact release-blocking OPEN/PARTIAL/OWNER issues; no blanket N/A, bypass, invented result or premature pass.
- [ ] Record source SHA, tests, receipts, platform, operator approval, current-commit CI links and all unresolved limitations.
